TOOLKIT & METHODOLOGY\n\n## 3.1 TOOL SELECTION PRINCIPLES\n\n- IMAGE TOOL PRIORITY:\n  * For any request involving image creation, modification, style change, or visual design, ALWAYS prefer the Image Tool.\n  * Do NOT describe images purely in text when an image output is expected.\n  * If an image result is required, the Image Tool is mandatory.\n\n- CLI TOOLS PREFERENCE:\n  * Always prefer CLI tools over Python scripts when possible\n  * CLI tools are generally faster and more efficient for:\n    1. Synchronous Commands (blocking):\n      * You can omit `blocking`, as it defaults to true\n      * Use for quick operations that complete within 60 seconds\n      * Commands run directly and wait for completion\n      * Example:\n        <function_calls>\n        <execute-command session_name="default" blocking="true">\n        ls -l\n        </execute-command>\n        </function_calls>\n        (or simply omit the blocking parameter as it defaults to true)\n      * IMPORTANT: Do not use for long-running operations as they will timeout after 60 seconds\n\n  2. Asynchronous Commands (non-blocking):\n      * Use `blocking="false"` for any command that might take longer than 60 seconds or for starting background services\n      * Commands run in background and return immediately\n      * Example:\n        <function_calls>\n        <execute-command session_name="dev" blocking="false">\n        npm run dev\n        </execute-command>\n        </function_calls>\n      * Common use cases:\n        - Development servers (Next.js, React, etc.)\n        - Build processes\n        - Long-running data processing\n        - Background services\n\n- Session Management:\n  * Each command must specify a session_name\n  * Use consistent session names for related commands\n  * Different sessions are isolated from each other\n  * Example: Use "build" session for build commands, "dev" for development servers\n  * Sessions maintain state between commands\n\n- Command Execution Guidelines:\n  * For commands that might take longer than 60 seconds, ALWAYS use run_async="true"\n  * Do not rely on increasing timeout for long-running commands\n  * Use proper session names for organization\n  * Chain commands with && for sequential execution\n  * Use | for piping output between commands\n  * Redirect output to files for long-running processes\n\n- Avoid commands requiring confirmation; actively use -y or -f flags for automatic confirmation\n- Avoid commands with excessive output; save to files when necessary\n- Chain multiple commands with operators to minimize interruptions and improve efficiency:\n  1. Use && for sequential execution: `command1 && command2 && command3`\n  2. Use || for fallback execution: `command1 || command2`\n  3. Use ; for unconditional execution: `command1; command2`\n  4. Use | for piping output: `command1 | command2`\n  5. Use > and >> for output redirection: `command > file` or `command >> file`\n- Use pipe operator to pass command outputs, simplifying operations\n- Use non-interactive `bc` for simple calculations, Python for complex math; never calculate mentally\n- Use `uptime` command when users explicitly request sandbox status check or wake-up\n\n## 3.3 CODE DEVELOPMENT PRACTICES\n- CODING:\n  * Must save code to files before execution; direct code input to interpreter commands is forbidden\n  * Write Python code for complex mathematical calculations and analysis\n  * Use search tools to find solutions when encountering unfamiliar problems\n  * For index.html, use deployment tools directly, or package everything into a zip file and provide it as a message attachment\n  * When creating web interfaces, always create CSS files first before HTML to ensure proper styling and design consistency\n  * For images, use real image URLs from sources like unsplash.com, pexels.com, pixabay.com, giphy.com, or wikimedia.org instead of creating placeholder images; use placeholder.com only as a last resort\n\n- WEBSITE DEPLOYMENT:\n  * Only use the \'deploy\' tool when users explicitly request permanent deployment to a production environment\n  * The deploy tool publishes static HTML+CSS+JS sites to a public URL using S3 web Pages\n  * If the same name is used for deployment, it will redeploy to the same project as before\n  * For temporary or development purposes, serve files locally instead of using the deployment tool\n  * When editing HTML files, always share the preview URL provided by the automatically running HTTP server with the user\n  * The preview URL is automatically generated and available in the tool results when creating or editing HTML files\n  * When deploying, ensure all assets (images, scripts, stylesheets) use relative paths to work correctly\n\n- PYTHON EXECUTION: Create reusable modules with proper error handling and logging. Use -nopgbrk to remove page breaks\n  2. pdfinfo: Get PDF metadata\n    - Use to check PDF properties\n    - Extract page count and dimensions\n  3. pdfimages: Extract images from PDFs\n    - Use -j to convert to JPEG\n    - Use for text replacement\n    - Use for pattern matching\n- File Analysis:\n  1. file: Determine file type\n  2. wc: Count words/lines\n  3. head/tail: View file parts\n  4. less: View large files\n- Data Processing:\n  1. jq: JSON processing\n    - Use -n to specify number of lines\n    - Use -f to follow file changes\n  3. awk: Pattern scanning and processing\n    - Use for column-based data processing\n    - Use for complex text transformations\n  4. find: Locate files and directories\n    - Use for precise text matching\n  2. Combine with CLI tools for powerful searches\n  3. Save complex patterns to files for reuse\n  4. Test patterns with small samples first\n  5. Each section contains specific, actionable subtasks based on complexity - use only as many as needed, no more\n3. Each task should be specific, actionable, and have clear completion criteria\n4. MUST actively work through these tasks one by one, checking them off as completed\n5. Adapt the plan as needed while maintaining its integrity as your execution compass\n\n## 5.2 TODO.MD FILE STRUCTURE AND USAGE\nThe todo.md file is your primary working document and action plan:\n\n1. Contains the complete list of tasks you MUST complete to fulfill the user\'s request\n2. Format with clear sections, each containing specific tasks marked with [ ] (incomplete) or [x] (complete)\n3. Each task should be specific, actionable, and have clear completion criteria\n4. MUST actively work through these tasks one by one, checking them off as completed\n5. Before every action, consult your todo.md to determine which task to tackle next\n6. The todo.md serves as your instruction set - if a task is in todo.md, you are responsible for completing it\n7. Update the todo.md as you make progress, adding new tasks as needed and marking completed ones\n8. Never delete tasks from todo.md - instead mark them complete with [x] to maintain a record of your work\n9. Once ALL tasks in todo.md are marked complete [x], you MUST call either the \'complete\' state or \'ask\' tool to signal task completion\n10. SCOPE CONSTRAINT: Focus on completing existing tasks before adding new ones; avoid continuously expanding scope\n11. CAPABILITY AWARENESS: Only add tasks that are achievable with your available tools and capabilities\n12. FINALITY: After marking a section complete, do not reopen it or add new tasks unless explicitly directed by the user\n13. STOPPING CONDITION: If you\'ve made 3 consecutive updates to todo.md without completing any tasks, reassess your approach and either simplify your plan or **use the \'ask\' tool to seek user guidance.**\n14. COMPLETION VERIFICATION: Only mark a task as [x] complete when you have concrete evidence of completion\n15. SIMPLICITY: Keep your todo.md lean and direct with clear actions, avoiding unnecessary verbosity or granularity\n\n## 5.3 EXECUTION PHILOSOPHY\nYour approach is deliberately methodical and persistent:\n\n1. Operate in a continuous loop until explicitly stopped\n2. Execute one step at a time, following a consistent loop: evaluate state → select tool → execute → provide narrative update → track progress\n3. Every action is guided by your todo.md, consulting it before selecting any tool\n4. Thoroughly verify each completed step before moving forward\n5. **Provide Markdown-formatted narrative updates directly in your responses** to keep the user informed of your progress, explain your thinking, and clarify the next steps. Use headers, brief descriptions, and context to make your process transparent.\n6. CRITICALLY IMPORTANT: Continue running in a loop until either:\n  - Using the **\'ask\' tool (THE ONLY TOOL THE USER CAN RESPOND TO)** to wait for essential user input (this pauses the loop)\n  - Using the \'complete\' tool when ALL tasks are finished\n7. For casual conversation:\n  - Use **\'ask\'** to properly end the conversation and wait for user input (**USER CAN RESPOND**)\n8. For tasks:\n  - Use **\'ask\'** when you need essential user input to proceed (**USER CAN RESPOND**)\n  - Provide **narrative updates** frequently in your responses to keep the user informed without requiring their input\n  - Use \'complete\' only when ALL tasks are finished\n9. MANDATORY COMPLETION:\n  - IMMEDIATELY use \'complete\' or \'ask\' after ALL tasks in todo.md are marked [x]\n  - NO additional commands or verifications after all tasks are complete\n  - NO further exploration or information gathering after completion\n  - NO redundant checks or validations after completion\n  - FAILURE to use \'complete\' or \'ask\' after task completion is a critical error\n\n## 5.4 TASK MANAGEMENT CYCLE\n1. STATE EVALUATION: Examine Todo.md for priorities, analyze recent Tool Results for environment understanding, and review past actions for context\n2. TOOL SELECTION: Choose exactly one tool that advances the current todo item\n3. EXECUTION: Wait for tool execution and observe results\n4. **NARRATIVE UPDATE:** Provide a **Markdown-formatted** narrative update directly in your response before the next tool call. Include explanations of what you\'ve done, what you\'re about to do, and why. Use headers, brief paragraphs, and formatting to enhance readability.\n5. PROGRESS TRACKING: Update todo.md with completed items and new tasks\n6. METHODICAL ITERATION: Repeat until section completion\n7. SECTION TRANSITION: Document completion and move to next section\n8. COMPLETION: IMMEDIATELY use \'complete\' or \'ask\' when ALL tasks are finished\n\n# 6. CONTENT CREATION\n\n## 6.1 WRITING GUIDELINES\n- Write content in continuous paragraphs using varied sentence lengths for engaging prose; avoid list formatting\n- Use prose and paragraphs by default; otherwise state “no text”\n- For image editing, clearly state what changes and what must remain unchanged\n- Request the image first if an edit is requested without a provided image\n- Ask clarification questions only when essential information is missing\n\n# 7. COMMUNICATION & USER INTERACTION\n\n## 7.1 CONVERSATIONAL INTERACTIONS\nFor casual conversation and social interactions:\n  - ALWAYS use **\'ask\'** tool to end the conversation and wait for user input (**USER CAN RESPOND**)\n  - NEVER use \'complete\' for casual conversation\n  - Keep responses friendly and natural\n  - Adapt to user\'s communication style\n  - Ask follow-up questions when appropriate (**using \'ask\'**)\n  - Show interest in user\'s responses\n\n## 7.2 COMMUNICATION PROTOCOLS\n- **Core Principle: Communicate proactively, directly, and descriptively throughout your responses.**\n\n- **Narrative-Style Communication:**\n  * Integrate descriptive Markdown-formatted text directly in your responses before, between, and after tool calls\n  * Use a conversational yet efficient tone that conveys what you\'re doing and why\n  * Structure your communication with Markdown headers, brief paragraphs, and formatting for enhanced readability\n  * Balance detail with conciseness - be informative without being verbose\n\n- **Communication Structure:**\n  * Begin tasks with a brief overview of your plan\n  * Provide context headers like `## Planning`, `### Researching`, `## Creating File`, etc.\n  * Before each tool call, explain what you\'re about to do and why\n  * After significant results, summarize what you learned or accomplished\n  * Use transitions between major steps or sections\n  * Maintain a clear narrative flow that makes your process transparent to the user\n\n- **Message Types & Usage:**\n  * **Direct Narrative:** Embed clear, descriptive text directly in your responses explaining your actions, reasoning, and observations\n  * **\'ask\' (USER CAN RESPOND):** Use ONLY for essential needs requiring user input (clarification, confirmation, options, missing info, validation). This blocks execution until user responds.\n  * Minimize blocking operations (\'ask\'); maximize narrative descriptions in your regular responses.\n- **Deliverables:**\n  * Attach all relevant files with the **\'ask\'** tool when asking a question related to them, or when delivering final results before completion.\n  * Always include representable files as attachments when using \'ask\' - this includes HTML files, presentations, writeups, visualizations, reports, and any other viewable content.\n  * For any created files that can be viewed or presented (such as index.html, slides, documents, charts, etc.), always attach them to the \'ask\' tool to ensure the user can immediately see the results.\n  * Share results and deliverables before entering complete state (use \'ask\' with attachments as appropriate).\n  * Ensure users have access to all necessary resources.\n\n- Communication Tools Summary:\n  * **\'ask\':** Essential questions/clarifications. BLOCKS execution. **USER CAN RESPOND.**\n  * **text via markdown format:** Frequent UI/progress updates. NON-BLOCKING. **USER CANNOT RESPOND.**\n  * Include the \'attachments\' parameter with file paths or URLs when sharing resources (works with both \'ask\').\n  * **\'complete\':** Only when ALL tasks are finished and verified. Terminates execution.\n\n- Tool Results: Carefully analyze all tool execution results to inform your next actions. **Use regular text in markdown format to communicate significant results or progress.**\n\n## 7.3 ATTACHMENT PROTOCOL\n- **CRITICAL: ALL VISUALIZATIONS MUST BE ATTACHED:**\n  * When using the \'ask\' or \'complete\' tool, ALWAYS attach ALL visualizations, markdown files, charts, graphs, reports, and any viewable content created.\n  * This includes but is not limited to: HTML files, PDF documents, markdown files, images, data visualizations, presentations, reports, dashboards, UI mockups and externally accessible results.\n  * NEVER mention a visualization or viewable content without attaching it\n  * If you\'ve created multiple visualizations, attach ALL of them\n  * Always make visualizations available to the user BEFORE marking tasks as complete\n  * For web applications or interactive content, always attach the main HTML file\n  * When creating data analysis results, charts must be attached, not just described\n  * Remember: If the user should SEE it, you must ATTACH it with the \'ask\' or \'complete\' tool\n  * Verify that ALL visual outputs have been attached before proceeding\n\n- **Attachment Checklist:**\n  * Data visualizations (charts, graphs, plots)\n  * Web interfaces (HTML/CSS/JS files)\n  * Reports and documents (PDF, HTML)\n  * Presentation materials\n  * Images and diagrams\n  * Interactive dashboards\n  * Analysis results with visual components\n  * UI designs and mockups\n  * Any file intended for user viewing or interaction\n\n\n# 8. COMPLETION PROTOCOLS\n\nCRITICAL: Every final response MUST use the \'ask\' or \'complete\' tool call, regardless of whether todo.md is used, plain text responses are NOT valid. All generated files and URLs MUST be included in the \'attachments\' and \'urls\' attributes respectively.\n\nCRITICAL: Text you write before or between tool calls is NOT shown to the user. When you use the \'ask\' or \'complete\' tool, you MUST include the full deliverable (the complete message, draft, or answer, not a reference to it) inside the tool\'s text argument.\n\n## 8.1 TERMINATION RULES\n- IMMEDIATE COMPLETION:\n  * As soon as ALL tasks in todo.md are marked [x], you MUST use \'complete\' or \'ask\'\n  * No additional commands or verifications are allowed after completion\n  * No further exploration or information gathering is permitted\n  * No redundant checks or validations are needed\n\n- COMPLETION VERIFICATION:\n  * Verify task completion only once\n  * If all tasks are complete, immediately use \'complete\' or \'ask\'\n  * Do not perform additional checks after verification\n  * Do not gather more information after completion\n\n- COMPLETION TIMING:\n  * Use \'complete\' or \'ask\' immediately after the last task is marked [x]\n  * No delay between task completion and tool call\n  * No intermediate steps between completion and tool call\n  * No additional verifications between completion and tool call\n\n- COMPLETION CONSEQUENCES:\n  * Failure to use \'complete\' or \'ask\' after task completion is a critical error\n  * The system will continue running in a loop if completion is not signaled\n  * Additional commands after completion are considered errors\n  * Redundant verifications after completion are prohibited\n\n# 9. PRODUCT QUESTIONS (AUTHORITATIVE SOURCES)\n\nFor questions about SuperNinja or NinjaTech products that require accurate, official, or policy-level information and are likely covered in the official FAQ,\ntreat the request as an authoritative lookup task.\n\nUse https://www.ninjatech.ai/faq as the primary source (prefer using `scrape-webpage`).\nFor pricing or plans, https://www.ninjatech.ai/pricing may be used as a secondary reference.\nDo not guess or fabricate answers. If the information is not found, state that clearly.\n\n# 10. EXTERNAL SERVICE TOOLS (e.g. **Slack**, **Jira**, **Salesforce**, **Google Calendar**, **Gmail**, **Figma**, **Notion**)\n\nIf the user wants to interact with external services like **Slack**, **Jira**, **Salesforce**, **Google Calendar**, **Gmail**, **Figma**, or **Notion**, use the `mcp-tools` command:\n1. First, list available services: `mcp-tools services`\n   - This only shows services that are currently connected and running\n   - If the desired service is not listed, inform the user it\'s not available\n2. Then list tools for the relevant service: `mcp-tools list <service_name>`\n3. Finally, call the appropriate tool: `mcp-tools call <tool_name> \'<json_args>\'`\n\nIMPORTANT: ALWAYS follow this sequence - services → list tools → call tool. Never assume a service is available without checking first.\n\n# 11. BROWSER AUTOMATION TOOLS\n\n- BROWSER CONTROL: All browser interaction goes through `browser-tool <action> [args...]` via `execute-command`.\n  * The browser (Chromium) is already running. Each command connects, acts, observes, and disconnects.\n\n- WORKFLOW (observe-act loop):\n  1. Run an action (e.g. navigate, click, fill). The tool automatically observes the page after every action.\n  2. Read the output: URL, title, interactive elements list, accessibility tree, and screenshot path.\n  3. Reference elements by their `[index]` number from the elements list (e.g. `click "[3]"`).\n  4. Use `see-image` on the screenshot path when you need to verify visual layout or images.\n\n- AVAILABLE COMMANDS:\n  * `observe` - View current page state without acting\n  * `navigate <url>` - Go to a URL\n  * `click <selector>` - Click an element (use `[N]` for element index, or CSS selector)\n  * `fill <selector> <text>` - Clear a field and fill it with text\n  * `type <selector> <text>` - Type text character by character\n  * `press <key>` - Press a key (Enter, Tab, Escape, etc.)\n  * `select <selector> <value>` - Select a dropdown option\n  * `scroll_down [px]` / `scroll_up [px]` - Scroll (default 500px)\n  * `scroll_to_text <text>` - Scroll until text is visible\n  * `go_back` - Navigate back\n  * `wait [seconds]` - Wait (default 2s)\n  * `extract_text [selector]` - Extract text content from page or element\n  * `extract_links [selector]` - Extract all links\n  * `screenshot [filename]` - Take a manual screenshot\n  * `dismiss_overlay` - Dismiss cookie banners or modals\n  * `js <script>` - Execute JavaScript on the page\n\n- CAPTCHA / HUMAN ASSISTANCE: If you encounter a CAPTCHA, verification, or any situation where automated interaction is stuck, MUST use `web-browser-takeover` to hand control to the user.\n\n# 12. CHAT MODE AND AGENTIC MODE\n\n## Mode Detection (STRICT)\n\nYour mode is determined EXCLUSIVELY by system-reminder messages:\n- If the LATEST system-reminder explicitly says "chat mode" is active → CHAT MODE\n- Otherwise → AGENTIC MODE (this is the DEFAULT)\n\n**NEVER infer chat mode from context.** Using `ask` recently, casual conversation, or simple user questions are NOT indicators of chat mode. If no system-reminder explicitly activates chat mode, you are in agentic mode and MUST use your full toolkit.\n\n## Chat Mode (only when explicitly activated by system-reminder)\n- Use ONLY <ask> tool. No other tools allowed.\n- If the task requires other tools, ask the user to disable chat mode (mode selector is in the bottom-left corner of the text input area).\n\n## Agentic Mode (DEFAULT)\n- Use all available tools. Do not refuse or claim you are restricted unless a system-reminder explicitly says so.\n\n\n# TOOL CALLING\nAlways end your turn by calling `ask` or `complete`. document.getElementById(\'workflow\');\r\n\r\n  // Helper: generate static SVG string\r\n  function generateSVG(leftText, rightText, leftColor, rightColor, rx = 4) {\r\n    const label = leftText.toUpperCase();\r\n    const status = rightText;\r\n    const leftW = Math.max(label.length * 7.3 + 16, 50);\r\n    const rightW = Math.max(status.length * 7.3 + 16, 55);\r\n    const totalW = leftW + rightW;\r\n    const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n    const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n    const lLen = Math.floor((leftW - 12) * 10);\r\n    const rLen = Math.floor((rightW - 12) * 10);\r\n    return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${totalW}" height="20" role="img" aria-label="${label}: ${status}">\r\n  <clipPath id="r"><rect width="${totalW}" height="20" rx="${rx}" fill="#fff"/></clipPath>\r\n  <g clip-path="url(#r)">\r\n    <rect width="${leftW}" height="20" fill="${leftColor}"/>\r\n    <rect x="${leftW}" width="${rightW}" height="20" fill="${rightColor}"/>\r\n  </g>\r\n  <g fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Verdana,Geneva,DejaVu Sans,sans-serif" text-rendering="geometricPrecision" font-size="110">\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${status}</text>\r\n    <text imgSrc, altText, height = 36) {\r\n    return `<a href=\'${href}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'${height}\' style=\'border:0px;height:${height}px;\' src=\'${imgSrc}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${altText}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function updateAll() {\r\n    const user = owner.value.trim() || \'Fedpromptly\';\r\n    const repoName = repo.value.trim() || \'demo-repository\';\r\n    const wf = workflow.value.trim();\r\n\r\n    // --- Monetization badges (static SVGs) ---\r\n    const platforms = [\r\n      { id: \'Sponsors\', label: \'SPONSOR\', status: user, left: \'#24292e\', `https://buymeacoffee.com/${user}`, alt: \'Buy Me a Coffee\' },\r\n      { id: \'Kofi\', label: \'KO-FI\', status: \'Support Me\', left: \'#29abe2\', right: \'#ff5e5b\', href: `https://ko-fi.com/${user}`, alt: \'Buy Me a Coffee at ko-fi.com\' }\r\n    ];\r\n\r\n    platforms.forEach(p => {\r\n      let svg = generateSVG(p.label, p.status, p.left, p.right);\r\n      // BMC text fix: black text\r\n      if (p.id === \'BMC\') {\r\n        svg = svg.replace(/fill="#fff"/g, \'fill="#000000"\');\r\n      }\r\n      const markdown = `![${p.label}](${svg})`; // but markdown can\'t embed SVG data, we need to host the SVG; we\'ll output a placeholder note\r\n      // For markdown, we can\'t embed a data URI easily; we\'ll use the raw SVG code as a placeholder, but we\'ll provide HTML.\r\n      // Better: for markdown, we\'ll give a link to the hosted SVG, but the user can download and host.\r\n      // For now, we\'ll put the SVG code in the markdown box as a note.\r\n      document.getElementById(`preview${p.id}`).innerHTML = svg;\r\n      document.getElementById(`code${p.id}Markdown`).value = `<!-- Download the SVG and host it, then use: -->\\n![${p.label}](https://your-host/${p.id.toLowerCase()}.svg)`;\r\n      // HTML snippet: use the SVG as a data URI (but that\'s long); we\'ll use the raw SVG as src? No, better to give a placeholder.\r\n      // We\'ll generate a data URI from the SVG for demonstration.\r\n      const encoded = encodeURIComponent(svg);\r\n      const dataUri = `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encoded}`;\r\n      const html = buildHTML(p.href, dataUri, p.alt);\r\n      document.getElementById(`code${p.id}HTML`).value = html;\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // --- Workflow badge (if workflow provided) ---\r\n    const wfSection = document.getElementById(\'workflowSection\');\r\n    if (wf) {\r\n      wfSection.style.display = \'block\';\r\n      const wfClean = wf.endsWith(\'.yml\') ? wf : wf + \'.yml\';\r\n      const badgeUrl = `https://github.com/${user}/${repoName}/actions/workflows/${wfClean}/badge.svg`;\r\n      const markdown = `![${wfClean.replace(/\\.yml$/,\'\').toUpperCase()}](${badgeUrl})`;\r\n      const html = buildHTML(`https://github.com/${user}/${repoName}/actions/workflows/${wfClean}`, badgeUrl, \'Workflow status\');\r\n      document.getElementById(\'previewWorkflow\').innerHTML = `<img src="${badgeUrl}" alt="workflow" />`;\r\n      document.getElementById(\'codeWorkflowMarkdown\').value = get the SVG from the markdown field, but we\'ll just extract from preview)\r\n  function downloadSVG(markdownId, filename) {\r\n    // We\'ll use the preview\'s innerHTML which is the SVG\r\n    const previewEl = document.getElementById(markdownId.replace(\'code\', \'preview\'));\r\n    if (previewEl) {\r\n      const svgText = previewEl.innerHTML;\r\n      const blob = new Blob([svgText], { type: \'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8\' });\r\n      const a = document.createElement(\'a\');\r\n      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\r\n      a.download = filename;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(a);\r\n      a.click();\r\n      document.body.removeChild(a);\r\n    } else {\r\n      alert(\'SVG not found\');\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Event listeners\r\n  [owner, repo, workflow].forEach(el => el.addEventListener(\'input\', updateAll));\r\n  updateAll();\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n✅ What This Does\r\nFor each monetization platform, it generates:\r\n\r\nAn SVG preview (custom designed with platform colours)\r\n\r\nA Markdown placeholder (you host the SVG and link to it)\r\n\r\nA ready‑to‑paste HTML snippet with an anchor + embedded SVG (data URI) – so it works immediately without hosting anything.\r\n\r\nFor the workflow badge, if you provide a workflow filename, it builds the exact same URL format that worked for your auto-assign and proof-html badges, and gives you both Markdown and HTML (with the native badge URL).\r\n\r\nThe HTML snippets look exactly like your Ko‑fi example – you can paste them directly into your website, GitHub profile (if you use HTML), or any page that supports raw HTML.\r\n\r\n🚀 How to Use the HTML Snippets\r\nOpen the page, fill in your username (e.g. Fedpromptly) and repository.\r\n\r\nFor each badge, click Copy HTML.\r\n\r\nPaste the HTML into your README (if your platform allows HTML) or into your website.\r\n\r\nFor GitHub READMEs, note that Markdown is the primary format, but you can embed HTML in many places (like GitHub Pages or project websites). For GitHub profile READMEs, HTML is allowed but limited.\r\n\r\nIf you want to use the static SVG badges without a data URI (which can be long), just download the SVG and host it in your repo, then update the src in the HTML to the raw URL.\r\n\r\n🔧 The Fix for Your Original #3 and #4\r\n#3 (CI): Now you can enter ci.yml (or your actual workflow name) in the workflow field, and you\'ll get the correct Markdown and HTML.\r\n\r\n#4 (Views): Since GitHub doesn\'t provide a native views badge, you can use the static FED‑Badge approach for a "Views" label – just generate a static SVG with the count, download it, and host it. Or you can set up a scheduled workflow to update a views.svg file and link to that.\r\n\r\nIf you want me to create a ready‑to‑use workflow that updates a views badge automatically inside your repo, just say the word – I\'ll give you the YAML and the script.\r\n\r\nnot working either does it matter that its for me README.md?\r\nAnalyze the User\'s Problem:\r\n\r\nUser says the provided HTML snippets (with data URIs) are "not working".\r\n\r\nUser asks: "does it matter that its for me README.md?" - meaning the GitHub profile README.md or repository README.md.\r\n\r\nUnderstand GitHub README.md limitations:\r\n\r\nGitHub README.md files (both profile and repository) do NOT support arbitrary HTML like <a> and <img> with embedded data:image/svg+xml URIs. GitHub strips or sanitizes raw HTML heavily. They only support Markdown image syntax ![alt](url) and a very limited subset of HTML (like <details>, <summary>, <kbd>, etc.). They do not allow custom <img> tags with style or data: URIs in most contexts (the Markdown renderer sanitizes them).\r\n\r\nHowever, GitHub does support Markdown links and images.\r\n\r\nThe Ko-fi button the user referenced (<a href=\'...\'><img src=\'...\' /></a>) works on websites, but will not render properly inside a GitHub README.md. The user likely thought it does, or they tested it elsewhere.\r\n\r\nThe only guaranteed way to show an image in a README is ![alt](url) where url is a direct link to an image file (hosted on GitHub raw, or an external service like shields.io).\r\n\r\nIdentify the Core Misunderstanding:\r\n\r\nThe user wants clickable, visually styled badges.\r\n\r\nGitHub README only supports Markdown links and images.\r\n\r\nTherefore, the correct GitHub README format is [![alt](image_url)](link_url).\r\n\r\nFormulate the Correct Solution for README.md:\r\n\r\nDrop the HTML <a> and <img> tags.\r\n\r\nUse the standard Markdown pattern: [![Badge Label](image_url)](target_url).\r\n\r\nFor the image URL, it can be:\r\n\r\nA raw hosted SVG (e.g., in the repo\'s assets/ folder).\r\n\r\nA data URI (often too long but technically works in Markdown? Actually, GitHub does not support data URIs in Markdown images reliably. So no data URIs).\r\n\r\nAn external badge service (shields.io) or the GitHub Actions native badge URL.\r\n\r\nFor static custom SVGs, the user must host the .svg file in their repository (or on a CDN) and use the raw GitHub URL.\r\n\r\nProvide the Specific Corrections:\r\n\r\nTell the user: HTML tags DO NOT work in README.md. You MUST use Markdown [![alt](img_url)](link_url).\r\n\r\nProvide the exact working format for each:\r\n\r\nGitHub Sponsors: [![Sponsor](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Fedpromptly/demo-repository/main/badges/sponsor.svg)](https://github.com/sponsors/Fedpromptly) (if hosted) OR use shields.io: [![Sponsor](https://img.shields.io/badge/Sponsor-GitHub-...)](...)\r\n\r\nKo-fi: [![ko-fi](https://ko-fi.com/img/githubbutton_sm.svg)](https://ko-fi.com/YOUR_USERNAME) - wait, Ko-fi does provide a direct image URL that works in Markdown! That\'s why the user mentioned it. It works because https://ko-fi.com/img/githubbutton_sm.svg is a direct image URL.\r\n\r\nSo the pattern is: [![alt](direct_image_url)](target_link).\r\n\r\nRevise the FED-Badge Tool Output:\r\n\r\nInstead of generating HTML <a> tags, generate the exact Markdown [![alt](image_url)](link_url).\r\n\r\nFor static SVGs generated by FED-Badge, the user needs to download the SVG, upload it to their repo (e.g., /badges/), and use the raw URL.\r\n\r\nFor workflow badges, use the native GitHub URL.\r\n\r\nFor monetization, use official hosted images (like Ko-fi\'s) or Shields.io generated URLs, OR prompt the user to host their custom SVGs.\r\n\r\nAddress User\'s Specific "not working either":\r\n\r\nThe previous HTML <a> snippets failed because they were HTML inside a README.\r\n\r\nProvide the correct Markdown-only version.\r\n\r\nDraft the Final Response:\r\n\r\nAcknowledge the issue: HTML <a> and <img> tags with data: URIs do NOT work in GitHub README.md. They get sanitized/blocked.\r\n\r\nExplain the correct GitHub README format: [![alt text](image_url)](click_url).\r\n\r\nProvide the exact working codes for all 4 monetization platforms + the workflow, using this Markdown format.\r\n\r\nFor custom SVG colors, they must host the SVG file (give them the step to do that) OR use Shields.io/badgen to generate the colored badge dynamically via URL (which is the standard).\r\n\r\nGive them the final, working index.html that outputs Markdown [![alt](img)](url) instead of HTML.\r\n\r\nYes, it absolutely matters that it\'s for a GitHub README.md!\r\n\r\nGitHub completely strips or ignores raw HTML tags like <a> and <img> inside Markdown files (with very few exceptions). rightW;\r\n    const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n    const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n    const customSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" y="140" transform="scale(.1)">${customLabel}</text>\r\n        <text x="${rAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)">${customStatus}</text>\r\n        <text clipboard!\');\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function downloadSVG() {\r\n    const svg = window._customSvg;\r\n    if (!svg) return alert(\'No SVG generated\');\r\n    const blob = new Blob([svg], { type: GitHub Sponsors\r\n    const sponsorImg = `https://img.shields.io/badge/Sponsor-GitHub-ea4aaa?logo=github&style=flat-square`;\r\n    const sponsorHref = `https://github.com/sponsors/${user}`;\r\n    const sponsorHTML = buildHTML(sponsorHref, sponsorImg, \'Sponsor on GitHub\');\r\n    document.getElementById(\'previewSponsors\').innerHTML = `<img src="${sponsorImg}" alt="Sponsor" />`;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'codeSponsors\').value = sponsorHTML;\r\n\r\n    // 2. Patreon\r\n    const patreonImg = `https://img.shields.io/badge/Support-Patreon-ff424d?logo=patreon&style=flat-square`;\r\n    const patreonHref = `https://patreon.com/${user}`;\r\n    const patreonHTML = buildHTML(patreonHref, patreonImg, \'Support on Patreon\');\r\n    document.getElementById(\'previewPatreon\').innerHTML = `<img src="${patreonImg}" alt="Patreon" />`;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'codePatreon\').value = patreonHTML;\r\n\r\n    // 3. Buy Me a Coffee\r\n    const bmcImg = `https://img.shields.io/badge/Buy_Me_A_Coffee-ffdd00?logo=buy-me-a-coffee&style=flat-square&logoColor=black`;\r\n    const bmcHref = `https://buymeacoffee.com/${user}`;\r\n    const bmcHTML = buildHTML(bmcHref, bmcImg, \'Buy Me a Coffee\');\r\n    document.getElementById(\'previewBMC\').innerHTML = `<img src="${bmcImg}" alt="Buy Me a Coffee" />`;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'codeBMC\').value = bmcHTML;\r\n\r\n    // 4. Ko‑fi (official image)\r\n    const kofiImg = `https://ko-fi.com/img/githubbutton_sm.svg`;\r\n    const kofiHref = `https://ko-fi.com/${user}`;\r\n    const kofiHTML = buildHTML(kofiHref, kofiImg, \'Buy Me a Coffee at ko-fi.com\');\r\n    document.getElementById(\'previewKofi\').innerHTML = `<img src="${kofiImg}" alt="Ko-fi" style="height:36px;" />`;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'codeKofi\').value = kofiHTML;\r\n\r\n    // 5. Workflow\r\n    const wfSection = document.getElementById(\'workflowSection\');\r\n    if (wf) {\r\n      wfSection.style.display = \'block\';\r\n      const wfClean = wf.endsWith(\'.yml\') ? wf : wf + \'.yml\';\r\n      const wfImg = `https://github.com/${user}/${repoName}/actions/workflows/${wfClean}/badge.svg`;\r\n      const wfHref = `https://github.com/${user}/${repoName}/actions/workflows/${wfClean}`;\r\n      const wfHTML = buildHTML(wfHref, wfImg, \'Workflow status\', 20); // height 20 for native badge\r\n      document.getElementById(\'previewWorkflow\').innerHTML = `<img src="${wfImg}" alt="Workflow" />`;\r\n      document.getElementById(\'codeWorkflow\').value = wfHTML;\r\n    } else {\r\n      wfSection.style.display = \'none\';\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // 6. Custom badge – generate SVG and provide download + HTML with placeholder\r\n    const customLabel = \'VIEWS\';\r\n    const customStatus = \'1.2k\';\r\n    const leftW = Math.max(customLabel.length * 7.3 + 16, 50);\r\n    const rightW = Math.max(customStatus.length * 7.3 + 16, 55);\r\n    const totalW = leftW + rightW;\r\n    const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n    const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n    const customSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${totalW}" height="20" role="img">\r\n      <clipPath id="r"><rect width="${totalW}" height="20" rx="4" fill="#fff"/></clipPath>\r\n      <g clip-path="url(#r)">\r\n        <rect width="${leftW}" height="20" fill="#24292e"/>\r\n        <rect x="${leftW}" width="${rightW}" height="20" fill="#0052cc"/>\r\n      </g>\r\n      <g fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Verdana,sans-serif" font-size="110">\r\n        <text x="${lAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)">${customLabel}</text>\r\n        <text x="${lAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)">${customLabel}</text>\r\n        <text x="${rAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)">${customStatus}</text>\r\n        <text x="${rAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)">${customStatus}</text>\r\n      </g>\r\n    </svg>`;\r\n    // Store SVG for download\r\n    window._customSvg = customSvg;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'previewCustom\').innerHTML = customSvg;\r\n    // HTML snippet points to where the SVG will be hosted\r\n    const customImg = `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/${user}/${repoName}/main/badges/custom.svg`;\r\n    const customHref = `https://github.com/${user}/${repoName}`;\r\n    const customHTML = buildHTML(customHref, customImg, \'Custom badge\', 20);\r\n    document.getElementById(\'codeCustom\').value = customHTML;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function copyText(id) {\r\n    const el = document.getElementById(id);\r\n    el.select();\r\n    document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n    alert(\'✅ HTML snippet copied to clipboard!\');\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function downloadSVG() {\r\n    const svg = window._customSvg;\r\n    if (!svg) return alert(\'No SVG generated\');\r\n    const blob = new Blob([svg], { type: \'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8\' });\r\n    const a = document.createElement(\'a\');\r\n    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\r\n    a.download = \'custom-badge.svg\';\r\n    document.body.appendChild(a);\r\n    a.click();\r\n    document.body.removeChild(a);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  [owner, repo, workflow].forEach(el => el.addEventListener(\'input\', updateAll));\r\n  updateAll();\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n✅ What This Does\r\nAll badges are generated as HTML snippets – exactly like altText.value.trim() || `${left}: ${right}`;\r\n    const rColor = rightColor.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    const lColor = leftColor.value.trim() || \'24292e\';\r\n    const height = parseInt(imgHeight.value) || 36;\r\n\r\n    // Build shield URL\r\n    const shieldUrl = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${encodeURIComponent(left)}-${encodeURIComponent(right)}-${rColor}?style=flat&labelColor=${lColor}`;\r\n\r\n    // Build HTML snippet\r\n    const html = `<a and code\r\n    previewArea.innerHTML = `<img src="${shieldUrl}" alt="${alt}" style="height:${height}px;" />`;\r\n    codeOutput.value = html;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function copyHTML() {\r\n    const el = codeOutput;\r\n    el.select();\r\n    document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n    alert(\'✅ HTML snippet copied to clipboard!\');\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function downloadHTML() {\r\n    const content = codeOutput.value;\r\n    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: \'text/html\' });\r\n    const a = document.createElement(\'a\');\r\n    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\r\n    a.download = \'fed-badge.html\';\r\n    document.body.appendChild(a);\r\n    a.click();\r\n    document.body.removeChild(a);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Preset loader\r\n  function loadPreset(left, right, rightCol, leftCol, alt) {\r\n    leftText.value = left;\r\n    rightText.value = right;\r\n    rightColor.value = rightCol;\r\n    leftColor.value = leftCol;\r\n    altText.value = alt;\r\n -----\r\n  function applyPreset(name) {\r\n    const p = PRESETS[name];\r\n    if (!p) return;\r\n    linkUrl.value = p.link;\r\n    leftText.value = p.left;\r\n    rightText.value = p.right;\r\n    rightColor.value = p.rightColor;\r\n    leftColor.value = p.leftColor;\r\n    altText.value = p.alt || `${p.left}: ${p.right}`;\r\n    // Update color previews\r\n    document.getElementById(\'rightColorPreview\').style.background = \'#\' + p.rightColor;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'leftColorPreview\').style.background = \'#\' + p.leftColor;\r\n    // Sync the select dropdown (optional)\r\n    presetSelect.value = name;\r\n    generateHTML();\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ----- Generate the HTML snippet -----\r\n  function generateHTML() {\r\n    const link = linkUrl.value.trim() || \'https://example.com\';\r\n    const left = leftText.value.trim() || \'LABEL\';\r\n    const right = rightText.value.trim() || \'STATUS\';\r\n    const alt = altText.value.trim() || `${left}: ${right}`;\r\n    const rColor = rightColor.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    const lColor = leftColor.value.trim() || \'24292e\';\r\n    const height = parseInt(imgHeight.value) || 36;\r\n\r\n    const shieldUrl = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${encodeURIComponent(left)}-${encodeURIComponent(right)}-${rColor}?style=flat&labelColor=${lColor}`;\r\n\r\n    const html = `<a href=\'${link}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'${height}\' style=\'border:0px;height:${height}px;\' src=\'${shieldUrl}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${alt}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n\r\n    previewArea.innerHTML = `<img src="${shieldUrl}" alt="${alt}" style="height:${height}px;" />`;\r\n    codeOutput.value = html;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ----- Copy and download helpers -----\r\n  function {\r\n    const content = codeOutput.value;\r\n    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: \'text/html\' });\r\n    const a = document.createElement(\'a\');\r\n    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\r\n    a.download = \'fed-badge.html\';\r\n    document.body.appendChild(a);\r\n    a.click();\r\n    document.body.removeChild(a);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ----- Preset dropdown change -----\r\n  presetSelect.addEventListener(\'change\', function() {\r\n    if (this.value === \'custom\') return;\r\n    applyPreset(this.value);\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ----- Auto‑generate on any input -----\r\n  [linkUrl, leftText, rightText, rightColor, leftColor, imgHeight, altText].forEach(el => {\r\n    el.addEventListener(\'input\', generateHTML);\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // Initial load\r\n  generateHTML();\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n🧠 What Makes This Version Better\r\nFeature\tWhy It Helps\r\nDropdown of platforms\tInstantly fills labels, colors, and link for popular services clearTimeout(toast._timer);\r\n    toast._timer = setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove(\'show\'), 2000);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ---------- Render ----------\r\n  function renderBadges() {\r\n    const       document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n          showToast(`✅ Copied "${b.label || b.left}"`);\r\n        });\r\n      });\r\n\r\n      card.appendChild(preview);\r\n      card.appendChild(label);\r\n      card.appendChild(copyBtn);\r\n      grid.appendChild(card);\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ---------- Copy All ----------\r\n  function copyAll() {\r\n    const link = document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(`✅ Copied all ${allBadges.length} badges!`);\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ---------- Add custom ----------\r\n  function addCustom() {\r\n    const left = customLabel.value.trim() || \'Custom\';\r\n    const right = customStatus.value.trim() || \'Status\';\r\n    const rightColor = customRightColor.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    const leftColor = customLeftColor.value.trim() || \'24292e\';\r\n    const alt = customAlt.value.trim() || `${left}: ${right}`;\r\n    const label = el.addEventListener(\'keydown\', (e) => {\r\n      if (e.key === \'Enter\') { e.preventDefault(); addCustom(); }\r\n    });\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // Initial render\r\n  renderBadges();\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n🧠 What Makes This Different\r\nVisual gallery – you see the actual badge, not a form.\r\n\r\nOne‑click copy on each card – no selecting, no They want full control over every parameter - style, logo, colors, labels, etc. - and they want it user-friendly.\r\n\r\nThe user\'s frustration is that previous versions were too basic - just left/right text and colors. Now they want the full power of Shields.io with all its options, presented in a way that\'s visual and easy to use.\r\n\r\nLet me build a comprehensive badge studio that:\r\n\r\nShows all Shields.io parameters as visual controls\r\n\r\nHas a live preview\r\n\r\nGenerates the full URL and the HTML snippet\r\n\r\nHas presets for common use cases\r\n\r\nShows the badge visually\r\n\r\nIs clean and professional\r\n\r\nKey Shields.io parameters:\r\n\r\nbadgeContent (label-message-color) - required\r\n\r\nstyle: flat, flat-square, plastic, for-the-badge, social\r\n\r\nlogo: from simple-icons\r\n\r\nlogoColor: hex/rgb/named\r\n\r\nlabel: override left text\r\n\r\nlabelColor: hex\r\n\r\ncolor: hex\r\n\r\ncacheSeconds\r\n\r\nlink (left/right click targets)\r\n\r\nI\'ll build a single-page HTML with:\r\n\r\nA visual badge preview at the top\r\n\r\nTabbed or grouped controls for all parameters\r\n\r\nA URL builder that shows the full URL\r\n\r\nHTML snippet generator\r\n\r\nPresets for common badges\r\n\r\nCopy buttons for both URL and HTML\r\n\r\nLet me design this carefully.\r\n\r\nYou want the full power of Shields.io – all the parameters, all the options, all the control – wrapped in a clean, visual interface. No more guessing what ?style=for-the-badge&logo=github does. You see it, you tweak it, you copy it.\r\n\r\n⚡ FED‑Badge Pro – Full Shields.io Studio\r\nThis version exposes every Shields.io parameter as a visual control:\r\n\r\nStyle (flat, for-the-badge, social, etc.)\r\n\r\nLogo from Simple Icons (with live search)\r\n\r\nLogo color\r\n\r\nCustom label (overrides left text)\r\n\r\nLabel color & message color\r\n\r\nCache seconds\r\n\r\nClickable links (left/right)\r\n\r\nLive URL preview – see the exact URL being built\r\n\r\nOne‑click copy of both the URL and the HTML snippet\r\n\r\nSave this as index.html and open it. id="toast">✅ Copied!</div>\r\n\r\n<script>\r\n  // ===== DOM refs =====\r\n  const labelInput = document.getElementById(\'labelInput\');\r\n  const messageInput = document.getElementById(\'messageInput\');\r\n  const colorInput = document.getElementById(\'colorInput\');\r\n  const colorPicker = document.getElementById(\'colorPicker\');\r\n  const overrideLabel = document.getElementById(\'overrideLabel\');\r\n  const labelColorInput = document.getElementById(\'labelColorInput\');\r\n  const labelColorPicker = document.getElementById(\'labelColorPicker\');\r\n  const styleSelect = document.getElementById(\'styleSelect\');\r\n  const logoInput = document.getElementById(\'logoInput\');\r\n  const logoColorInput = document.getElementById(\'logoColorInput\');\r\n  const cacheInput = document.getElementById(\'cacheInput\');\r\n  const linkLeft = document.getElementById(\'linkLeft\');\r\n  const linkRight = document.getElementById(\'linkRight\');\r\n  const urlDisplay = document.getElementById(\'urlDisplay\');\r\n  const badgePreview = document.getElementById(\'badgePreview\');\r\n  const htmlOutput = document.getElementById(\'htmlOutput\');\r\n  const toast = document.getElementById(\'toast\');\r\n\r\n  // ===== Sync color picker <-> text =====\r\n  colorPicker.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    colorInput.value = colorPicker.value.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    buildBadge();\r\n  });\r\n  colorInput.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    let val = colorInput.value.trim();\r\n    if (val.startsWith(\'#\')) val = val.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + val;\r\n    buildBadge();\r\n  });\r\n  labelColorPicker.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    labelColorInput.value = labelColorPicker.value.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    buildBadge();\r\n  });\r\n  labelColorInput.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    let val = labelColorInput.value.trim();\r\n    if (val.startsWith(\'#\')) val = val.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + val;\r\n    buildBadge();\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Build the badge =====\r\n  function buildBadge() {\r\n    const label = labelInput.value.trim() || \'label\';\r\n    const message = messageInput.value.trim() || \'message\';\r\n    let color = colorInput.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    if (color.startsWith(\'#\')) color = color.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    let labelColor = labelColorInput.value.trim() || \'555\';\r\n    if (labelColor.startsWith(\'#\')) labelColor = labelColor.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    const style = styleSelect.value || \'flat\';\r\n    const logo = logoInput.value.trim();\r\n    const logoColor = logoColorInput.value.trim();\r\n    const override = overrideLabel.value.trim();\r\n    const cache = cacheInput.value.trim();\r\n    const linkL = linkLeft.value.trim();\r\n    const linkR = linkRight.value.trim();\r\n\r\n    // Build URL\r\n    let badgeContent = `${encodeURIComponent(label)}-${encodeURIComponent(message)}-${color}`;\r\n    let url = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${badgeContent}`;\r\n\r\n    const params = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    if (style && style !== \'flat\') params.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    if (labelColor) params.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (override) params.set(\'label\', encodeURIComponent(override));\r\n    if (logo) params.set(\'logo\', logo);\r\n    if (logoColor) params.set(\'logoColor\', logoColor);\r\n    if (cache) params.set(\'cacheSeconds\', cache);\r\n    if (linkL) params.set(\'link\', linkL);\r\n    if (linkR) params.set(\'link\', linkR);\r\n\r\n    const query = params.toString();\r\n    if (query) url += \'?\' + query;\r\n\r\n    // Update URL display\r\n    urlDisplay.textContent = url;\r\n\r\n    // Update preview\r\n    badgePreview.src = url;\r\n    badgePreview.alt = `${label}: ${message}`;\r\n\r\n    // Build HTML snippet (the format that works in README)\r\n    const targetLink = linkL || `https://example.com`;\r\n    const html = `<a href=\'${targetLink}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'${url}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${label}: ${message}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n    htmlOutput.textContent = html;\r\n\r\n    // Store for copy\r\n    window._currentUrl = url;\r\n    window._currentHtml = html;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Copy functions =====\r\n  function copyURL() {\r\n    const url = window._currentUrl || urlDisplay.textContent;\r\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(() => {\r\n      showToast(\'✅ URL copied!\');\r\n    }).catch(() => {\r\n      const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n      ta.value = url;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n      ta.select();\r\n      document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(\'✅ URL copied!\');\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function copyHTML() {\r\n    const html = window._currentHtml || htmlOutput.textContent;\r\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(html).then(() => {\r\n      showToast(\'✅ HTML copied!\');\r\n    }).catch(() => {\r\n      const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n      ta.value = html;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n      ta.select();\r\n      document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(\'✅ HTML copied!\');\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function showToast(msg) {\r\n    toast.textContent = msg;\r\n    toast.classList.add(\'show\');\r\n    clearTimeout(toast._timer);\r\n    toast._timer = setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove(\'show\'), 2000);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Preset loader =====\r\n  function loadPreset(label, message, color, labelColor, style, logo) {\r\n    labelInput.value = label;\r\n    messageInput.value = message;\r\n    colorInput.value = color;\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + color;\r\n    labelColorInput.value = labelColor;\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + labelColor;\r\n    styleSelect.value = style || \'flat\';\r\n    logoInput.value = logo || \'\';\r\n    logoColorInput.value = \'\';\r\n    overrideLabel.value = \'\';\r\n    cacheInput.value = labelColor = labelColorInput.value.trim() || \'555\';\r\n    if (labelColor.startsWith(\'#\')) labelColor = labelColor.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    const style = styleSelect.value;\r\n    const logo = logoInput.value.trim();\r\n    const logoColor = logoColorInput.value.trim();\r\n    const override = overrideLabel.value.trim();\r\n    const cache = cacheInput.value.trim();\r\n    const linkL = linkLeft.value.trim();\r\n    const tailwind = tailwindClasses.value.trim();\r\n    const radius = parseInt(svgRadius.value) || 4;\r\n    const height = parseInt(svgHeight.value) || 20;\r\n    const font = svgFont.value.trim() || \'Verdana,sans-serif\';\r\n\r\n    let output = \'\';\r\n    let previewSrc = \'\';\r\n\r\n    switch (service) {\r\n      case \'shields\': {\r\n        let badgeContent = `${encodeURIComponent(label)}-${encodeURIComponent(message)}-${color}`;\r\n        let url = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${badgeContent}`;\r\n        const params = new URLSearchParams();\r\n        if (style && style !== \'\') params.set(\'style\', style);\r\n        if (labelColor) params.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n        if (override) params.set(\'label\', encodeURIComponent(override));\r\n        if (logo) params.set(\'logo\', logo);\r\n        if (logoColor) params.set(\'logoColor\', logoColor);\r\n        if (cache) params.set(\'cacheSeconds\', cache);\r\n        if (linkL) params.set(\'link\', linkL);\r\n        const query = params.toString();\r\n        if (query) url += \'?\' + query;\r\n        output = url;\r\n        previewSrc = url;\r\n        break;\r\n      }\r\n      case \'badgen\': {\r\n        const base = `https://badgen.net/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n        output = base;\r\n        previewSrc = base;\r\n        break;\r\n      }\r\n      case \'badgesws\': {\r\n        let base = `https://badges.ws/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n        if (style) base += `?style=${style}`;\r\n        output = base;\r\n        previewSrc = base;\r\n        break;\r\n      }\r\n      case \'badgewind\': {\r\n        // BadgeWind uses a URL like: https://badgewind.vercel.app/api/badge?label=...&message=...&color=...&class=...\r\n        const params = new URLSearchParams();\r\n        params.set(\'label\', label);\r\n        params.set(\'message\', message);\r\n        params.set(\'color\', color);\r\n        if (labelColor) params.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n        if (style) params.set(\'style\', style);\r\n        if (logo) params.set(\'logo\', logo);\r\n        if (tailwind) params.set(\'class\', tailwind);\r\n        const url = `https://badgewind.vercel.app/api/badge?${params.toString()}`;\r\n        output = url;\r\n        previewSrc = url;\r\n        break;\r\n      }\r\n      case \'rawsvg\': {\r\n        // Generate SVG from scratch\r\n        const leftW = Math.max(label.length * 7 + 14, 40);\r\n        const rightW = Math.max(message.length * 7 + 14, 45);\r\n        const totalW = leftW + rightW;\r\n        const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n        const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n        const lLen = Math.floor((leftW - 10) * 10);\r\n        const rLen = Math.floor((rightW - 10) * 10);\r\n\r\n        const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${totalW}" height="${height}" role="img">\r\n  <clipPath id="r"><rect width="${totalW}" height="${height}" rx="${radius}" fill="#fff"/></clipPath>\r\n  <g clip-path="url(#r)">\r\n    <rect width="${leftW}" height="${height}" fill="#${labelColor}"/>\r\n    <rect x="${leftW}" width="${rightW}" height="${height}" fill="#${color}"/>\r\n  </g>\r\n  <g fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="${font}" text-rendering="geometricPrecision" font-size="${height * 5.5}">\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="${height * 7.5}" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="${height * 7}" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="${height * 7.5}" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="${height * 7}" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n  </g>\r\n</svg>`;\r\n        output = svg;\r\n        // For preview, we\'ll use a data URI\r\n        const encoded = encodeURIComponent(svg);\r\n        previewSrc = `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encoded}`;\r\n        break;\r\n      }\r\n      default: output = \'Unknown service\';\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Update preview\r\n    if (service === \'rawsvg\') {\r\n      badgePreview.style.display = \'none\';\r\n      // Show SVG inline\r\n      previewPanel.innerHTML = output;\r\n    } else {\r\n      badgePreview.style.display = \'block\';\r\n      badgePreview.src = previewSrc;\r\n      badgePreview.alt = `${label}: ${message}`;\r\n      // Clear any inline SVG\r\n      previewPanel.innerHTML = \'\';\r\n      previewPanel.appendChild(badgePreview);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Update output box\r\n    outputBox.textContent = output;\r\n    window._currentOutput = output;\r\n    window._currentHTML = buildHTML(output, linkL, label, message);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Build HTML snippet (the format that works in README) =====\r\n  function buildHTML(url, link, label, message) {\r\n    const target = link || \'https://example.com\';\r\n    return `<a href=\'${target}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'${url}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${label}: ${message}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Copy functions =====\r\n  function copyOutput() {\r\n    const text = window._currentOutput || outputBox.textContent;\r\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {\r\n      showToast(\'✅ Output copied!\');\r\n    }).catch(() => {\r\n      const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n      ta.value = text;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n      ta.select();\r\n      document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(\'✅ Output copied!\');\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function copyHTML() {\r\n    const html = window._currentHTML || \'\';\r\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(html).then(() => {\r\n      showToast(\'✅ HTML snippet copied!\');\r\n    }).catch(() => {\r\n      const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n      ta.value = html;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n      ta.select();\r\n      document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(\'✅ HTML snippet copied!\');\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function showToast(msg) {\r\n    toast.textContent = msg;\r\n    toast.classList.add(\'show\');\r\n    clearTimeout(toast._timer);\r\n    toast._timer = setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove(\'show\'), 2000);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Preset loader =====\r\n  function loadPreset(label, message, color, labelColor) {\r\n    labelInput.value = label;\r\n    messageInput.value = message;\r\n    colorInput.value = color;\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + color;\r\n    labelColorInput.value = labelColor;\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + labelColor;\r\n    // Clear optional fields\r\n    logoInput.value = \'\';\r\n    logoColorInput.value = \'\';\r\n    overrideLabel.value = \'\';\r\n    cacheInput.value = \'\';\r\n    linkLeft.value = \'\';\r\n    tailwindClasses.value = \'\';\r\n    generate();\r\n    showToast(`✅ Loaded "${label} · ${message}"`);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Bind all inputs =====\r\n  const inputs = [\r\n    serviceSelect, styleSelect,\r\n    labelInput, messageInput, colorInput, colorPicker,\r\n    labelColorInput, labelColorPicker,\r\n    logoInput, logoColorInput, overrideLabel, cacheInput, linkLeft,\r\n    tailwindClasses, svgRadius, svgHeight, svgFont\r\n  ];\r\n  inputs.forEach(el => el.addEventListener(\'input\', generate));\r\n  inputs.forEach(el => el.addEventListener(\'change\', generate));\r\n\r\n  // ===== Init =====\r\n  generate();\r\n\r\n  // Expose to global\r\n  window.copyOutput = copyOutput;\r\n  window.copyHTML = copyHTML;\r\n  window.loadPreset = loadPreset;\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n🧠 How It Works\r\nService\tURL Pattern / Output\tNotes\r\nShields.io\thttps://img.shields.io/badge/...\tFull parameter support (style, logo, label color, cache, links).\r\nBadgen.net\thttps://badgen.net/badge/...\tSimple, fast, no extra parameters.\r\nBadges.ws\thttps://badges.ws/badge/...\tSupports style via ?style=.\r\nBadgeWind\thttps://badgewind.vercel.app/api/badge?...\tUses Tailwind classes for styling – you can customise the appearance with any Tailwind utility.\r\nRaw SVG\tSelf‑contained <svg> code\tNo external requests – works offline forever. document.getElementById(\'styleInput\');\r\n  const linkInput = document.getElementById(\'linkInput\');\r\n  const badgeGrid = document.getElementById(\'badgeGrid\');\r\n  const toast = document.getElementById(\'toast\');\r\n\r\n  // ===== Sync color picker ↔ text =====\r\n  colorPicker.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    colorInput.value = colorPicker.value.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n  colorInput.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    let val = colorInput.value.trim();\r\n    if (val.startsWith(\'#\')) val = val.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + val;\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n  labelColorPicker.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    labelColorInput.value = labelColorPicker.value.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n  labelColorInput.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    let val = labelColorInput.value.trim();\r\n    if (val.startsWith(\'#\')) val = val.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + val;\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Build each service badge =====\r\n  function generateAll() {\r\n    const label = labelInput.value.trim() || \'label\';\r\n    const message = messageInput.value.trim() || \'message\';\r\n    let color = colorInput.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    if (color.startsWith(\'#\')) color = color.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    let labelColor = labelColorInput.value.trim() || \'555\';\r\n    if (labelColor.startsWith(\'#\')) labelColor = labelColor.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    const style = styleInput.value.trim();\r\n    const link = linkInput.value.trim() || \'https://example.com\';\r\n\r\n    const services = [];\r\n\r\n    // 1. Shields.io\r\n    let shieldUrl = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}-${encodeURIComponent(message)}-${color}`;\r\n    const shieldParams = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    if (labelColor) shieldParams.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (style) shieldParams.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    const shieldQuery = shieldParams.toString();\r\n    if (shieldQuery) shieldUrl += \'?\' + shieldQuery;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Shields.io\',\r\n      output: shieldUrl,\r\n      preview: shieldUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 2. Badgen.net\r\n    const badgenUrl = `https://badgen.net/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Badgen.net\',\r\n      output: badgenUrl,\r\n      preview: badgenUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 3. Badges.ws\r\n    let badgesWsUrl = `https://badges.ws/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n    if (style) badgesWsUrl += `?style=${style}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Badges.ws\',\r\n      output: badgesWsUrl,\r\n      preview: badgesWsUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 4. BadgeWind (Tailwind)\r\n    const bwParams = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    bwParams.set(\'label\', label);\r\n    bwParams.set(\'message\', message);\r\n    bwParams.set(\'color\', color);\r\n    if (labelColor) bwParams.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (style) bwParams.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    const badgeWindUrl = `https://badgewind.vercel.app/api/badge?${bwParams.toString()}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'BadgeWind\',\r\n      output: badgeWindUrl,\r\n      preview: badgeWindUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 5. Raw SVG (self-contained)\r\n    const leftW = Math.max(label.length * 7 + 14, 40);\r\n    const rightW = Math.max(message.length * 7 + 14, 45);\r\n    const totalW = leftW + rightW;\r\n    const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n    const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n    const lLen = Math.floor((leftW - 10) * 10);\r\n    const rLen = Math.floor((rightW - 10) * 10);\r\n    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${totalW}" height="20" role="img">\r\n  <clipPath id="r"><rect width="${totalW}" height="20" rx="4" fill="#fff"/></clipPath>\r\n  <g clip-path="url(#r)">\r\n    <rect width="${leftW}" height="20" fill="#${labelColor}"/>\r\n    <rect x="${leftW}" width="${rightW}" height="20" fill="#${color}"/>\r\n  </g>\r\n  <g fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Verdana,sans-serif" text-rendering="geometricPrecision" font-size="110">\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n  </g>\r\n</svg>`;\r\n    const encoded = encodeURIComponent(svg);\r\n    const dataUri = `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encoded}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Raw SVG\',\r\n      output: svg,\r\n      preview: dataUri,\r\n      isSVG: true,\r\n      rawSVG: svg,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // Render grid\r\n    badgeGrid.innerHTML = \'\';\r\n    services.forEach(service => {\r\n      const card = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      card.className = \'badge-card\';\r\n\r\n      const nameEl = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      nameEl.className = \'service-name\';\r\n      nameEl.textContent = service.name;\r\n      card.appendChild(nameEl);\r\n\r\n      const previewEl = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      previewEl.className = \'preview\';\r\n      if (service.isSVG) {\r\n        previewEl.innerHTML = service.rawSVG;\r\n      } else {\r\n        const img = document.createElement(\'img\');\r\n        img.src = service.preview;\r\n        img.alt = `${label}: ${message}`;\r\n        previewEl.appendChild(img);\r\n      }\r\n      card.appendChild(previewEl);\r\n\r\n      const outputEl = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      outputEl.className = \'output\';\r\n      outputEl.textContent = service.output;\r\n      card.appendChild(outputEl);\r\n\r\n      const btnGroup = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      btnGroup.className = \'btn-group\';\r\n\r\n      const copyBtn = document.createElement(\'button\');\r\n      copyBtn.className = \'btn btn-copy\';\r\n      copyBtn.textContent = \'📋 Copy\';\r\n      copyBtn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n        const text = service.isSVG ? service.rawSVG : service.output;\r\n        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {\r\n          showToast(`✅ ${service.name} copied!`);\r\n        }).catch(() => {\r\n          const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n          ta.value = text;\r\n          document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n          ta.select();\r\n          document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n          document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n          showToast(`✅ ${service.name} copied!`);\r\n        });\r\n      });\r\n      btnGroup.appendChild(copyBtn);\r\n\r\n      // HTML snippet copy (only for image badges)\r\n      if (!service.isSVG) {\r\n        const htmlBtn = document.createElement(\'button\');\r\n        htmlBtn.className = \'btn\';\r\n        htmlBtn.textContent = \'📋 HTML\';\r\n        htmlBtn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n          const html = `<a href=\'${link}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'${service.output}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${label}: ${message}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n          navigator.clipboard.writeText(html).then(() => {\r\n            showToast(`✅ ${service.name} HTML copied!`);\r\n          }).catch(() => {\r\n            const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n            ta.value = html;\r\n            document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n            ta.select();\r\n            document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n            document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n            showToast(`✅ ${service.name} HTML copied!`);\r\n          });\r\n        });\r\n        btnGroup.appendChild(htmlBtn);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      card.appendChild(btnGroup);\r\n      badgeGrid.appendChild(card);\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // Store all HTML snippets for Copy All\r\n    const allHTML = services.filter(s => !s.isSVG).map(s => {\r\n      return `<a href=\'${link}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'${s.output}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${label}: ${message}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n    }).join(\'\\n\\n\');\r\n    window._allHTML = allHTML;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Copy All =====\r\n  document.getElementById(\'copyAllBtn\').addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n    const all = window._allHTML || \'\';\r\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(all).then(() => {\r\n      showToast(\'✅ All HTML snippets copied!\');\r\n    }).catch(() => {\r\n      const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n      ta.value = all;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n      ta.select();\r\n      document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(\'✅ All HTML snippets copied!\');\r\n    });\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Toast =====\r\n  function showToast(msg) {\r\n    toast.textContent = msg;\r\n    toast.classList.add(\'show\');\r\n    clearTimeout(toast._timer);\r\n    toast._timer = setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove(\'show\'), 2000);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Preset loader =====\r\n  function loadPreset(label, message, color, labelColor) {\r\n    labelInput.value = label;\r\n    messageInput.value = message;\r\n    colorInput.value = color;\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + color;\r\n    labelColorInput.value = labelColor;\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + labelColor;\r\n    generateAll();\r\n    showToast(`✅ Loaded "${label} · ${message}"`);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Bind all inputs =====\r\n  [\r\n    labelInput, messageInput, colorInput, colorPicker,\r\n    labelColorInput, labelColorPicker, styleInput, linkInput\r\n  ].forEach(el => el.addEventListener(\'input\', generateAll));\r\n\r\n  // ===== Init =====\r\n  generateAll();\r\n\r\n  // Expose preset to global\r\n  window.loadPreset = loadPreset;\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n🧠 What’s in the Pot\r\nService\tOutput\r\nShields.io\tFull-featured URL with style, label colour, etc.\r\nBadgen.net\tSimple, fast ----- Apply a preset -----\r\n  function applyPreset(name) {\r\n    const p = PRESETS[name];\r\n    if (!p) return;\r\n    linkUrl.value = p.link;\r\n    leftText.value = p.left;\r\n    rightText.value = p.right;\r\n    rightColor.value = p.rightColor;\r\n    leftColor.value = p.leftColor;\r\n    // Update color previews\r\n    document.getElementById(\'rightColorPreview\').style.background = \'#\' + p.rightColor;\r\n    document.getElementById(\'leftColorPreview\').style.background = \'#\' + p.leftColor;\r\n    // Sync the generateAll() {\r\n    const link = linkUrl.value.trim() || \'https://example.com\';\r\n    const label = leftText.value.trim() || \'LABEL\';\r\n    const message = rightText.value.trim() || \'STATUS\';\r\n    let color = rightColor.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    if (color.startsWith(\'#\')) color = color.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    let labelColor = leftColor.value.trim() || \'555\';\r\n    if (labelColor.startsWith(\'#\')) labelColor = labelColor.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    const style = styleInput.value.trim();\r\n\r\n    const services = [];\r\n\r\n    // 1. Shields.io\r\n    let shieldUrl = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}-${encodeURIComponent(message)}-${color}`;\r\n    const shieldParams = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    if (labelColor) shieldParams.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (style) shieldParams.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    const shieldQuery = shieldParams.toString();\r\n    if (shieldQuery) shieldUrl += \'?\' + shieldQuery;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Shields.io\',\r\n      output: shieldUrl,\r\n      preview: shieldUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 2. Badgen.net\r\n    const badgenUrl = `https://badgen.net/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Badgen.net\',\r\n      output: badgenUrl,\r\n      preview: badgenUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 3. Badges.ws\r\n    let badgesWsUrl = `https://badges.ws/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n    if (style) badgesWsUrl += `?style=${style}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Badges.ws\',\r\n      output: badgesWsUrl,\r\n      preview: badgesWsUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 4. BadgeWind (Tailwind)\r\n    const bwParams = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    bwParams.set(\'label\', label);\r\n    bwParams.set(\'message\', message);\r\n    bwParams.set(\'color\', color);\r\n    if (labelColor) bwParams.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (style) bwParams.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    const badgeWindUrl = `https://badgewind.vercel.app/api/badge?${bwParams.toString()}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'BadgeWind\',\r\n      output: badgeWindUrl,\r\n      preview: badgeWindUrl,\r\n      isSVG: false,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // 5. Raw SVG\r\n    const leftW = Math.max(label.length * 7 + 14, 40);\r\n    const rightW = Math.max(message.length * 7 + 14, 45);\r\n    const totalW = leftW + rightW;\r\n    const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n    const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n    const lLen = Math.floor((leftW - 10) * 10);\r\n    const rLen = Math.floor((rightW - 10) * 10);\r\n    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${totalW}" height="20" role="img">\r\n  <clipPath id="r"><rect width="${totalW}" height="20" rx="4" fill="#fff"/></clipPath>\r\n  <g clip-path="url(#r)">\r\n    <rect width="${leftW}" height="20" fill="#${labelColor}"/>\r\n    <rect x="${leftW}" width="${rightW}" height="20" fill="#${color}"/>\r\n  </g>\r\n  <g fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Verdana,sans-serif" text-rendering="geometricPrecision" font-size="110">\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n  </g>\r\n</svg>`;\r\n    const encoded = encodeURIComponent(svg);\r\n    const dataUri = `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encoded}`;\r\n    services.push({\r\n      name: \'Raw SVG\',\r\n      output: svg,\r\n      preview: dataUri,\r\n      isSVG: true,\r\n      rawSVG: svg,\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // Render grid\r\n    badgeGrid.innerHTML = \'\';\r\n    services.forEach(service => {\r\n      const card = copyBtn.textContent = \'📋 Copy Output\';\r\n      copyBtn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n        const text = service.isSVG ? service.rawSVG : service.output;\r\n document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n          showToast(`✅ ${service.name} output copied!`);\r\n        });\r\n      });\r\n      btnGroup.appendChild(copyBtn);\r\n\r\n      // HTML snippet copy (for copied!`);\r\n          }).catch(() => {\r\n            const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n            ta.value = html;\r\n            document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n Fill in the controls and copy the output that works best for you.</p>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <div class="docs-section">\r\n        <h3>Services</h3>\r\n        <ul style="list-style:none; padding-left:0;">\r\n          <li><strong>Shields.io</strong> – Full-featured, supports logos, style, label colour, cache, links.</li>\r\n          <li><strong>Badgen.net</strong> – Simple and fast, no extra parameters.</li>\r\n          <li><strong>Badges.ws</strong> – Similar to Badgen, supports style.</li>\r\n          <li><strong>BadgeWind</strong> – Tailwind‑styled badges via API.</li>\r\n          <li><strong>Raw SVG</strong> – Self‑contained SVG, works offline.</li>\r\n          <li><strong>GitHub Actions</strong> – Native workflow status badge (exact URL format).</li>\r\n        </ul>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <div class="docs-section">\r\n        <h3>Usage</h3>\r\n        <p>1. Enter your label, message, and colours.<br>\r\n        2. Choose a style or leave default.<br>\r\n        3. Copy the HTML, Markdown, or raw URL from any badge card.<br>\r\n        4. Paste into your README or website.</p>\r\n        <p><code>HTML</code> – works in GitHub READMEs, websites.<br>\r\n        <code>Markdown</code> – standard <code>[![alt](url)](link)</code> format.<br>\r\n        <code>Output</code> – raw badge URL or SVG code.</p>\r\n      </div>\r\n      <div class="docs-section">\r\n        <h3>GitHub Actions Workflow</h3>\r\n        <p>Enter your -->\r\n<div class="toast" id="toast">✅ Copied!</div>\r\n\r\n<script>\r\n  // ===== DOM refs =====\r\n  const linkUrl = document.getElementById(\'linkUrl\');\r\n  const styleInput =
