rightColor.value = colorPicker.value.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n  rightColor.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    let val = rightColor.value.trim();\r\n    if (val.startsWith(\'#\')) val = val.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + val;\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n  labelColorPicker.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    leftColor.value = labelColorPicker.value.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n  leftColor.addEventListener(\'input\', () => {\r\n    let val = leftColor.value.trim();\r\n    if (val.startsWith(\'#\')) val = val.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + val;\r\n    generateAll();\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Presets =====\r\n  const PRESETS = {\r\n    \'github-sponsors\': { link: \'https://github.com/sponsors/YOUR_USERNAME\', left: \'Sponsor\', right: \'GitHub\', rightColor: \'ea4aaa\', leftColor: \'24292e\' },\r\n    \'patreon\': { link: left: \'Ko-fi\', right: \'Support Me\', rightColor: \'ff5e5b\', leftColor: \'29abe2\' },\r\n    \'discord\': { link: \'https://discord.gg/YOUR_INVITE\', left: \'Discord\', right: \'Join Server\', rightColor: \'5865f2\', leftColor: \'24292e\' },\r\n    \'fedos\': { link: \'https://fedpromptly.com\', left: \'FED-OS\', right: \'Active\', rightColor: \'0052cc\', leftColor: \'24292e\' },\r\n    \'dpromptly\': { link: \'https://fedpromptly.com\', left: \'Network\', right: \'DPromptly\', rightColor: \'cc317c\', leftColor: \'24292e\' },\r\n    \'embeddable\': { link: \'https://fedpromptly.com\', left: \'Code\', right: \'Embeddable\', rightColor: \'2ea043\', leftColor: \'24292e\' },\r\n    \'community\': { link: \'https://fedpromptly.com\', left: \'Community\', right: \'Join Server\', rightColor: \'5865f2\', leftColor: \'24292e\' },\r\n    \'build-passing\': { link: \'https://example.com\', left: \'Build\', right: \'Passing\', rightColor: \'brightgreen\', leftColor: \'555\' },\r\n    \'coverage\': { link: \'https://example.com\', left: \'Coverage\', right: \'100%\', rightColor: \'green\', leftColor: \'555\' },\r\n    \'license\': { link: \'https://example.com\', left: \'License\', right: \'MIT\', rightColor: \'blue\', leftColor: \'555\' },\r\n    \'version\': { link: \'https://example.com\', left: \'Version\', right: \'v1.0.0\', rightColor: \'orange\', leftColor: \'555\' }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  function applyPreset(name) {\r\n    const p = PRESETS[name];\r\n    if (!p) return;\r\n    linkUrl.value = p.link;\r\n    leftText.value = p.left;\r\n    rightText.value = p.right;\r\n    rightColor.value = p.rightColor;\r\n    leftColor.value = p.leftColor;\r\n    colorPicker.value = \'#\' + p.rightColor;\r\n    labelColorPicker.value = \'#\' + p.leftColor;\r\n    // Switch to generator tab\r\n    switchTab(\'generator\');\r\n    generateAll();\r\n    showToast(`✅ Loaded "${p.left} · ${p.right}"`);\r\n  }\r\n  window.applyPreset = applyPreset;\r\n\r\n  // ===== Generate all badges =====\r\n  function generateAll() {\r\n    const link = linkUrl.value.trim() || \'https://example.com\';\r\n    const label = leftText.value.trim() || \'LABEL\';\r\n    const message = rightText.value.trim() || \'STATUS\';\r\n    let color = rightColor.value.trim() || \'0052cc\';\r\n    if (color.startsWith(\'#\')) color = color.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    let labelColor = leftColor.value.trim() || \'555\';\r\n    if (labelColor.startsWith(\'#\')) labelColor = labelColor.replace(\'#\', \'\');\r\n    const style = styleInput.value.trim();\r\n    const repo = repoInput.value.trim() || \'owner/repo\';\r\n    const workflow = workflowInput.value.trim() || \'ci.yml\';\r\n\r\n    const services = [];\r\n\r\n    // 1. Shields.io\r\n    let shieldUrl = `https://img.shields.io/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}-${encodeURIComponent(message)}-${color}`;\r\n    const shieldParams = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    if (labelColor) shieldParams.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (style) shieldParams.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    const shieldQuery = shieldParams.toString();\r\n    if (shieldQuery) shieldUrl += \'?\' + shieldQuery;\r\n    services.push({ name: \'Shields.io\', output: shieldUrl, preview: shieldUrl, isSVG: false });\r\n\r\n    // 2. Badgen.net\r\n    const badgenUrl = `https://badgen.net/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n    services.push({ name: \'Badgen.net\', output: badgenUrl, preview: badgenUrl, isSVG: false });\r\n\r\n    // 3. Badges.ws\r\n    let badgesWsUrl = `https://badges.ws/badge/${encodeURIComponent(label)}/${encodeURIComponent(message)}/${color}`;\r\n    if (style) badgesWsUrl += `?style=${style}`;\r\n    services.push({ name: \'Badges.ws\', output: badgesWsUrl, preview: badgesWsUrl, isSVG: false });\r\n\r\n    // 4. BadgeWind\r\n    const bwParams = new URLSearchParams();\r\n    bwParams.set(\'label\', label);\r\n    bwParams.set(\'message\', message);\r\n    bwParams.set(\'color\', color);\r\n    if (labelColor) bwParams.set(\'labelColor\', labelColor);\r\n    if (style) bwParams.set(\'style\', style);\r\n    const badgeWindUrl = `https://badgewind.vercel.app/api/badge?${bwParams.toString()}`;\r\n    services.push({ name: \'BadgeWind\', output: badgeWindUrl, preview: badgeWindUrl, isSVG: false });\r\n\r\n    // 5. Raw SVG\r\n    const leftW = Math.max(label.length * 7 + 14, 40);\r\n    const rightW = Math.max(message.length * 7 + 14, 45);\r\n    const totalW = leftW + rightW;\r\n    const lAnchor = Math.floor((leftW / 2) * 10);\r\n    const rAnchor = Math.floor((leftW + (rightW / 2)) * 10);\r\n    const lLen = Math.floor((leftW - 10) * 10);\r\n    const rLen = Math.floor((rightW - 10) * 10);\r\n    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${totalW}" height="20" role="img">\r\n  <clipPath id="r"><rect width="${totalW}" height="20" rx="4" fill="#fff"/></clipPath>\r\n  <g clip-path="url(#r)">\r\n    <rect width="${leftW}" height="20" fill="#${labelColor}"/>\r\n    <rect x="${leftW}" width="${rightW}" height="20" fill="#${color}"/>\r\n  </g>\r\n  <g fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Verdana,sans-serif" text-rendering="geometricPrecision" font-size="110">\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${lAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${lLen}">${label}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="150" fill="#010101" fill-opacity=".3" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n    <text x="${rAnchor}" y="140" transform="scale(.1)" textLength="${rLen}">${message}</text>\r\n  </g>\r\n</svg>`;\r\n    const encoded = encodeURIComponent(svg);\r\n    const dataUri = `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encoded}`;\r\n    services.push({ name: \'Raw SVG\', output: svg, preview: dataUri, isSVG: true, rawSVG: svg });\r\n\r\n    // 6. GitHub Actions Workflow\r\n    const workflowUrl = `https://github.com/${repo}/actions/workflows/${workflow}/badge.svg`;\r\n    services.push({ name: \'GitHub Actions\', output: workflowUrl, preview: workflowUrl, isSVG: false });\r\n\r\n    // Render grid\r\n    badgeGrid.innerHTML = \'\';\r\n    services.forEach(service => {\r\n      const card = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      card.className = \'badge-card\';\r\n\r\n      const nameEl = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      nameEl.className = \'service-name\';\r\n      nameEl.textContent = service.name;\r\n      card.appendChild(nameEl);\r\n\r\n      const previewEl = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      previewEl.className = \'preview\';\r\n      if (service.isSVG) {\r\n        previewEl.innerHTML = service.rawSVG;\r\n      } else {\r\n        const img = document.createElement(\'img\');\r\n        img.src = service.preview;\r\n        img.alt = `${label}: ${message}`;\r\n        previewEl.appendChild(img);\r\n      }\r\n      card.appendChild(previewEl);\r\n\r\n      const outputEl = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      outputEl.className = \'output\';\r\n      outputEl.textContent = service.isSVG ? service.rawSVG : service.output;\r\n      card.appendChild(outputEl);\r\n\r\n      const btnGroup = document.createElement(\'div\');\r\n      btnGroup.className = \'btn-group\';\r\n\r\n      // Copy Output (URL or SVG)\r\n      const copyBtn = document.createElement(\'button\');\r\n      copyBtn.className = \'btn btn-copy\';\r\n      copyBtn.textContent = \'📋 Output\';\r\n      copyBtn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n        const text = service.isSVG ? service.rawSVG : service.output;\r\n        copyText(text, `${service.name} output`);\r\n      });\r\n      btnGroup.appendChild(copyBtn);\r\n\r\n      // Copy HTML (only for image badges)\r\n      if (!service.isSVG) {\r\n        const htmlBtn = document.createElement(\'button\');\r\n        htmlBtn.className = \'btn\';\r\n        htmlBtn.textContent = \'📋 HTML\';\r\n        htmlBtn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n          const html = `<a href=\'${link}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'${service.output}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${label}: ${message}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n          copyText(html, `${service.name} HTML`);\r\n        });\r\n        btnGroup.appendChild(htmlBtn);\r\n\r\n        // Copy Markdown\r\n        const mdBtn = document.createElement(\'button\');\r\n        mdBtn.className = \'btn btn-markdown\';\r\n        mdBtn.textContent = \'📝 Markdown\';\r\n        mdBtn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n          const md = `[![${label}: ${message}](${service.output})](${link})`;\r\n          copyText(md, `${service.name} Markdown`);\r\n        });\r\n        btnGroup.appendChild(mdBtn);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      card.appendChild(btnGroup);\r\n      badgeGrid.appendChild(card);\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // Store all HTML and Markdown for Copy All\r\n    const allHtml = services.filter(s => !s.isSVG).map(s => {\r\n      return `<a href=\'${link}\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'${s.output}\' border=\'0\' alt=\'${label}: ${message}\' />\r\n</a>`;\r\n    }).join(\'\\n\\n\');\r\n    const allMd = services.filter(s => !s.isSVG).map(s => {\r\n      return `[![${label}: ${message}](${s.output})](${link})`;\r\n    }).join(\'\\n\\n\');\r\n    window._allHtml = allHtml;\r\n    window._allMd = allMd;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Copy helper with toast =====\r\n  function copyText(text, label) {\r\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {\r\n      showToast(`✅ ${label || \'Copied\'}!`);\r\n    }).catch(() => {\r\n      const ta = document.createElement(\'textarea\');\r\n      ta.value = text;\r\n      document.body.appendChild(ta);\r\n      ta.select();\r\n      document.execCommand(\'copy\');\r\n      document.body.removeChild(ta);\r\n      showToast(`✅ ${label || \'Copied\'}!`);\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  function showToast(msg) {\r\n    toast.textContent = msg;\r\n    toast.classList.add(\'show\');\r\n    clearTimeout(toast._timer);\r\n    toast._timer = setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove(\'show\'), 2000);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Copy All buttons =====\r\n  document.getElementById(\'copyAllHtmlBtn\').addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n    const html = window._allHtml || \'\';\r\n    copyText(html, \'All HTML snippets\');\r\n  });\r\n  document.getElementById(\'copyAllMarkdownBtn\').addEventListener(\'click\', () => {\r\n    const md = window._allMd || \'\';\r\n    copyText(md, \'All Markdown snippets\');\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Tab switching =====\r\n  function switchTab(tabId) {\r\n    document.querySelectorAll(\'.tab-content\').forEach(el => el.classList.remove(\'active\'));\r\n    document.querySelectorAll(\'.header nav button\').forEach(el => el.classList.remove(\'active\'));\r\n    document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add(\'active\');\r\n    document.querySelector(`.header nav button[data-tab="${tabId}"]`).classList.add(\'active\');\r\n  }\r\n  document.querySelectorAll(\'.header nav button\').forEach(btn => {\r\n    btn.addEventListener(\'click\', () => switchTab(btn.dataset.tab));\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Bind inputs =====\r\n  [linkUrl, styleInput, leftText, rightText, rightColor, leftColor, colorPicker, labelColorPicker, repoInput, workflowInput].forEach(el => {\r\n    el.addEventListener(\'input\', generateAll);\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // ===== Init =====\r\n  generateAll();\r\n</script>\r\n</body>\r\n</html>\r\n🧠 What’s No installation, no dependencies.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 📸 Screenshots\r\n\r\n*(Add your own screenshots here – the tool generates a clean dark‑themed UI.)*\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 📦 Services Supported\r\n\r\n| Service | URL Pattern | Notes |\r\n| :--- | :--- | :--- |\r\n| **Shields.io** | `https://img.shields.io/badge/...` | Full parameter support (style, logo, label colour, cache, links). |\r\n| **Badgen.net** | `https://badgen.net/badge/...` | Simple and fast. |\r\n| **Badges.ws** | `https://badges.ws/badge/...` | Supports style via query. |\r\n| **BadgeWind** | `https://badgewind.vercel.app/...` | Tailwind‑styled badges. |\r\n| **Raw SVG** | Self‑contained `<svg>` | No external requests – works offline. |\r\n| **GitHub Actions** | `https://github.com/owner/repo/actions/workflows/.../badge.svg` | Native workflow status. |\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 🛠️ Usage Examples\r\n\r\n### HTML (works in GitHub READMEs)\r\n```html\r\n<a href=\'https://fedpromptly.com\' target=\'_blank\'>\r\n    <img height=\'36\' style=\'border:0px;height:36px;\' src=\'https://img.shields.io/badge/FED--OS-Active-0052cc?style=flat&labelColor=24292e\' border=\'0\' alt=\'FED-OS: Active\' />\r\n</a>\r\nMarkdown (standard)\r\nmarkdown\r\n[![FED-OS: Active](https://img.shields.io/badge/FED--OS-Active-0052cc?style=flat&labelColor=24292e)](https://fedpromptly.com)\r\nRaw SVG (copy the <svg> code)\r\nsvg\r\n<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="110" height="20" role="img">\r\n  ...\r\n</svg>\r\n🤝 Contributing\r\nWe welcome contributions! We welcome all types of contributions – bug reports, feature requests, documentation improvements, and code patches.\r\n\r\n## How to Contribute\r\n\r\n1. **Fork** the repository.\r\n2. **Clone** your fork:\r\n   ```bash\r\n   git clone https://github.com/your-username/fed-badge.git\r\nCreate a branch for your feature or fix:\r\n\r\nbash\r\ngit checkout -b feature/your-feature\r\nMake your changes – ensure the tool still works (it\'s a single HTML file, so test it locally).\r\n\r\nCommit your changes with a descriptive message:\r\n\r\nbash\r\ngit commit -m "Add: description of your change"\r\nPush to your fork:\r\n\r\nbash\r\ngit push origin feature/your-feature\r\nOpen a Pull Request against the main branch.\r\n\r\nCode Style\r\nHTML: use consistent indentation (2 spaces).\r\n\r\nCSS: follow the existing naming conventions (BEM-ish).\r\n\r\nJavaScript: use const/let, avoid globals.\r\n\r\nReporting Issues\r\nUse the GitHub Issues page.\r\n\r\nInclude a clear Folder Scanner (with Demo Mode)\r\njavascript\r\nconst DEMO_DATA = {\r\n  files: [\r\n    { path: \'src/main.py\', ext: \'.py\', size: 1024, lines: 200 },\r\n    { path: \'src/utils.py\', ext: \'.py\', size: 512, lines: 80 },\r\n    { path: \'tests/test_main.py\', ext: \'.py\', size: 256, lines: 40 },\r\n    { path: \'README.md\', ext: \'.md\', size: 128, lines: 20 },\r\n    { path: \'package.json\', ext: \'.json\', size: 64, lines: 15 }\r\n  ],\r\n  stats: { totalFiles: 5, totalLines: 355, languages: { Python: 320, JSON: 15, Markdown: 20 } }\r\n};\r\n\r\nasync function scanFolder(folder) {\r\n  const files = [];\r\n  const queue = [{ handle: folder, path: \'\' }];\r\n  \r\n  while (queue.length > 0 && files.length < CONFIG.maxFiles) {\r\n    const { handle, path } = queue.shift();\r\n    \r\n    for await (const entry of handle.values()) {\r\n      const entryPath = path + \'/\' + entry.name;\r\n      if (shouldIgnore(entryPath)) continue;\r\n      \r\n      if (entry.kind === \'file\') {\r\n        const file = await entry.getFile();\r\n        if (shouldSkipFile(file)) continue;\r\n        files.push({\r\n          path: entryPath,\r\n          name: entry.name,\r\n          size: file.size,\r\n          ext: getExtension(entry.name),\r\n          lines: await countLines(file)\r\n        });\r\n      } else if (entry.kind === \'directory\' && queue.length < CONFIG.maxFiles) {\r\n        queue.push({ handle: entry, path: entryPath });\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  return files;\r\n}\r\n2. Canvas Animator (Cascade Mode)\r\njavascript\r\nclass CascadeAnimator {\r\n  constructor(canvas, files) {\r\n    this.canvas = canvas;\r\n    this.ctx = canvas.getContext(\'2d\');\r\n    this.files = files;\r\n    this.drops = files.map((file, i) => ({\r\n      text: file.name,\r\n      x: Math.random() * canvas.width,\r\n      y: Math.random() * canvas.height - canvas.height,\r\n      speed: 0.5 + Math.random() * 1.5,\r\n      color: getColorForExtension(file.ext)\r\n    }));\r\n  }\r\n  \r\n  render(time) {\r\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\r\n    ctx.fillStyle = this.bgColor;\r\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);\r\n    \r\n    this.drops.forEach(drop => {\r\n      drop.y += drop.speed;\r\n      if (drop.y > this.canvas.height) {\r\n        drop.y = -20;\r\n        drop.x = Math.random() * this.canvas.width;\r\n      }\r\n      ctx.fillStyle = drop.color;\r\n      ctx.font = `${this.fontSize}px monospace`;\r\n      ctx.fillText(drop.text, drop.x, drop.y);\r\n    });\r\n    \r\n    if (this.isPlaying) {\r\n      requestAnimationFrame(() => this.render(performance.now()));\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n3. GIF Export (with Web Worker)\r\njavascript\r\n// main.js\r\nasync function exportGIF(frames, delay) {\r\n  const worker = new Worker(\'gif-worker.js\');\r\n  \r\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\r\n    worker.postMessage({ frames, delay, width: 800, height: 200 });\r\n    worker.onmessage = (e) => {\r\n      if (e.data.progress) {\r\n        updateProgress(e.data.progress);\r\n      } else if (e.data.blob) {\r\n        resolve(e.data.blob);\r\n        worker.terminate();\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n    worker.onerror = reject;\r\n  });\r\n}\r\njavascript\r\n// gif-worker.js\r\nimportScripts(\'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gif.js@0.2.0/dist/gif.worker.js\');\r\n\r\nself.onmessage = function(e) {\r\n  const { frames, delay, width, height } = e.data;\r\n  const gif = new GIF({ width, height, workers: 2 });\r\n  \r\n  frames.forEach((frame, i) => {\r\n    gif.addFrame(frame, { delay: delay * 1000 });\r\n    self.postMessage({ progress: (i + 1) / frames.length });\r\n  });\r\n  \r\n  gif.on(\'finished\', (blob) => {\r\n    self.postMessage({ blob });\r\n  });\r\n  \r\n  gif.render();\r\n};\r\n✅ Final Checklist\r\nFeature\tStatus\r\nDemo Mode\t✅\r\nFolder Scanner (with limits)\t✅\r\nBrowser Fallback\t✅\r\nPlay/Pause/Stop\t✅\r\nSpeed Slider\t✅\r\nColor Pickers\t✅\r\nTheme Presets\t✅\r\nGIF Export (Web Worker)\t✅\r\nProgress Indicator\t✅\r\nStatic ASCII Export\t✅\r\nSmart Copy (with backlink)\t✅\r\nLocalStorage Persistence\t✅\r\nError Handling\t✅\r\nResponsive Design\t✅\r\nThis is now a production-ready, delightful, and performant animation studio. EXPORT OPTIONS (MORE)\r\nExport as WebM (MP4)\r\nUse MediaRecorder API to export as high-quality MP4:\r\n\r\njavascript\r\nfunction exportAsMP4(canvas) {\r\n  const stream = canvas.captureStream(30); // 30 fps\r\n  const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: \'video/webm\' });\r\n  recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {\r\n    const blob = e.data;\r\n    downloadBlob(blob, \'banner.webm\');\r\n  };\r\n  recorder.start();\r\n  setTimeout(() => recorder.stop(), 10000);\r\n}\r\nExport as APNG\r\nAnimations with transparency and better quality than GIF.\r\n\r\nExport as Terminal Sequence\r\nExport as a .txt file with ANSI escape codes so it can be played directly in a terminal.\r\n\r\nbash\r\ncat banner.ansi | python -c "import sys; print(sys.stdin.read())"\r\n🌐 6. SHARING & INTEGRATION\r\nShareable URL\r\nGenerate a shareable link that encodes all settings (mode, colors, size) as query parameters.\r\n\r\ntext\r\nhttps://fed-os.github.io/FED-Bages/?mode=cascade&bg=0d1117&text=00ff41&size=800x200\r\nEmbed Widget\r\nGenerate an <iframe> code that embeds the live animation on any website.\r\n\r\nhtml\r\n<iframe src="https://fed-os.github.io/FED-Bages/embed?repo=https://github.com/user/repo" width="800" height="200"></iframe>\r\nSocial Cards\r\nWhen someone shares the link, generate an OG image with the animation preview.\r\n\r\n🏷️ 7.
